2450: /* Upload the guest texture for a VP draw into a per-frame texture slot
2451:  * (re-uploaded every frame: gcm/cube's plasma animates in guest memory).
2452:  * Returns the slot index (SRV at heap 1+slot) or -1. Must run while the
2453:  * command list is open, before the draw passes. */
2454: /* NV4097 TEXTURE_CONTROL1 component remap -> D3D12 Shader4ComponentMapping.
2455:  * The crossbar decode itself is RSX semantics and lives in
2456:  * rsx_texture_layout.c; all that is left here is packing the four selectors
2457:  * into D3D12's word. Its force-zero/force-one encodings are 4 and 5, which is
2458:  * what RSX_REMAP_ZERO/ONE already are, so the selectors pass straight through.
2459:  *
2460:  * out[] arrives in the crossbar's field order A,R,G,B; D3D12 wants
2461:  * destR | destG<<3 | destB<<6 | destA<<9 | valid<<12. */
2462: static u32 rsx_remap_to_d3d(u32 c1, u32 basef)
2463: {
2464:     u8 out[4];
2465:     rsx_texture_component_remap(c1, basef, out);
2466:     /* TEX_REMAP_ID=<n>: override the derived crossbar with a fixed mapping, to
2467:      * test channel order directly. The resource holds the guest's A8R8G8B8
2468:      * bytes straight through, so its components are (R=A, G=R, B=G, A=B) and
2469:      * the shader needs destR=1, destG=2, destB=3, destA=0 to see real RGBA.
2470:      *   1 = identity   2 = rotate (the ARGB fix)   3 = BGRA swap */
2471:     { static int fixed = -1;
2472:       if (fixed < 0) { const char* e = getenv("TEX_REMAP_ID"); fixed = e ? atoi(e) : 0; }
2473:       if (fixed == 1) return 0u | (1u<<3) | (2u<<6) | (3u<<9) | (1u<<12);
2474:       if (fixed == 2) return 1u | (2u<<3) | (3u<<6) | (0u<<9) | (1u<<12);
2475:       if (fixed == 3) return 2u | (1u<<3) | (0u<<6) | (3u<<9) | (1u<<12); }
2476:     /* D3D12 mapping: destR | destG<<3 | destB<<6 | destA<<9 | valid bit */
2477:     { static int _rd = -1; if (_rd < 0) _rd = getenv("REMAPDBG") ? 1 : 0;
2478:       if (_rd) { static u32 seen[32]; static int ns=0;
2479:         u32 k = (basef << 16) | (c1 & 0xFFFFu); int f=0;
2480:         for (int i2=0;i2<ns;i2++) if (seen[i2]==k) f=1;
2481:         if (!f && ns<32) { seen[ns++]=k;
2482:             fprintf(stderr, "[REMAP] basef=0x%02X c1=0x%04X -> destR=%u destG=%u destB=%u destA=%u%c",
2483:                     basef, c1 & 0xFFFFu, out[1], out[2], out[3], out[0], 10); } } }
2484:     return out[1] | (out[2] << 3) | (out[3] << 6) | (out[0] << 9) | (1u << 12);
2485: }

2487: /* Morton/Z-order texel offset for RSX swizzled textures (LN bit clear).
2488:  * Interleaves x (even bit positions) and y (odd) until the smaller dimension's
2489:  * bits run out, then the larger dimension's remaining bits ride above -- the
2490:  * NV40 layout (matches RPCS3 convert_linear_swizzle). POT dims only, which is
2491:  * what the hardware requires for swizzled textures. */
2492: 
2493: /* Sparse FNV-1a over a texture's source bytes -- enough to notice an animated
2494:  * surface changing, cheap enough to run on every bind. Kept a function rather
2495:  * than a statement-expression macro so the file still compiles under MSVC;
2496:  * ps3recomp builds with clang-cl, but titles like Tokyo Jungle drive the build
2497:  * through the Visual Studio generator. */
2498: static u32 tex_csum(const u8* base, u32 nbytes)
2499: {
2500:     u32 h = 2166136261u;
2501:     u32 step = nbytes > 4096u ? nbytes / 1024u : 4u;
2502:     for (u32 i = 0; i + 3 < nbytes; i += step) {
2503:         u32 w32; memcpy(&w32, base + i, sizeof w32);
2504:         h ^= w32; h *= 16777619u;
2505:     }
2506:     return h;
2507: }
2508: 
2509: static int vp_upload_tex_slot(u32 off, u32 w, u32 h, u32 fmt, int cube, u32 mips)
2510: {
2511:     /* TEX_BUDGET=<n>: cap texture uploads per frame. Off by default -- it was
2512:      * tried against the TDR and does not help, because PERF shows the upload
2513:      * path costing 0.01s for 775 calls. Kept because it is the right lever if a
2514:      * title ever does hit an upload-bound frame. */
2515:     { static int budget = -1;
2516:       if (budget < 0) { const char* e = getenv("TEX_BUDGET"); budget = e ? atoi(e) : 0; }
2517:       if (budget > 0) {
2518:           static u64 last_frame = ~0ull; static int used = 0;
2519:           if (last_frame != (u64)s_d3d.frame_count) { last_frame = (u64)s_d3d.frame_count; used = 0; }
2520:           if (++used > budget) return -1;   /* try again next frame */
2521:       } }
2522:     extern uint8_t* vm_base;
2523: 
2524:     if (!off || !w || !h || !vm_base || !s_d3d.srv_heap) return -1;
2525:     /* Format classes (base = fmt & 0x9F). The LBP loading screen uses:
2526:      * 0x85 A8R8G8B8 (swizzled UI art), 0x8B G8B8 (the 1024x2048 linear FONT
2527:      * atlas -- without it no text renders at all), 0x86/87/88 DXT1/23/45
2528:      * (512x512 detail/LUT layers bound at t1/t3 on every draw). */
2529:     /* Format class, row sizes and swizzling are RSX semantics: rsx_texture_
2530:      * layout.c owns them so the Metal backend can read a guest texture without
2531:      * re-deriving any of it. Only the DXGI mapping below is ours. */
2532:     rsx_tex_layout tl;
2533:     rsx_texture_layout(fmt, w, h, &tl);
2534:     u32 basef = fmt & 0x9F;              /* still needed for the SRV remap */
2535:     int argb = (tl.fmt == RSX_TEXFMT_R8G8B8A8);
2536:     int dxt  = tl.compressed;
2537:     /* Compressed rows are counted in blocks, so keep bpp at 1 for the byte
2538:      * arithmetic the diagnostics below do. */
2539:     u32 bpp = tl.compressed ? 1u : tl.bytes_per_texel;
2540:     /* The DECODED image's format, which is not always the guest's: the packed
2541:      * 16-bit colour formats arrive here unpacked to RGBA8, and DEPTH24_D8 as a
2542:      * float. rsx_texture_layout.h says which class each format byte lands in. */
2543:     DXGI_FORMAT dxfmt = (tl.fmt == RSX_TEXFMT_R8G8B8A8) ? DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM
2544:                       : (tl.fmt == RSX_TEXFMT_R8G8)     ? DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8_UNORM
2545:                       : (tl.fmt == RSX_TEXFMT_BC1)      ? DXGI_FORMAT_BC1_UNORM
2546:                       : (tl.fmt == RSX_TEXFMT_BC2)      ? DXGI_FORMAT_BC2_UNORM
2547:                       : (tl.fmt == RSX_TEXFMT_BC3)      ? DXGI_FORMAT_BC3_UNORM
2548:                       : (tl.fmt == RSX_TEXFMT_R16)      ? DXGI_FORMAT_R16_UNORM
2549:                       : (tl.fmt == RSX_TEXFMT_R16G16)   ? DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16_UNORM
2550:                       : (tl.fmt == RSX_TEXFMT_R16G16F)  ? DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16_FLOAT
2551:                       : (tl.fmt == RSX_TEXFMT_R16G16B16A16F) ? DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT
2552:                       : (tl.fmt == RSX_TEXFMT_R32F)     ? DXGI_FORMAT_R32_FLOAT
2553:                       : (tl.fmt == RSX_TEXFMT_R32G32B32A32F) ? DXGI_FORMAT_R32G32B32A32_FLOAT
2554:                       : DXGI_FORMAT_R8_UNORM;
2555:     /* DXT data is stored as linear 4x4 block rows (compressed formats are
2556:      * never Morton-swizzled on RSX). Row of blocks = (w/4)*blocksize. */
2557:     u32 blkrow  = dxt ? tl.row_bytes : 0;
2558:     u32 blkrows = dxt ? tl.rows      : 0;
2559:     const u32 key_off = off;         /* lookup key: the offset as bound */
2560:     /* Sparse checksum of a texture's source bytes -- enough to notice an
2561:      * animated surface changing, cheap enough to run on every bind. */
2562:     /* (portable helper tex_csum() -- see above; a statement-expression macro
2563:      * here was a GCC/Clang extension MSVC rejects.) */
2564:     #define TEX_CSUM(base, nbytes) tex_csum((base), (nbytes))
2565:     int slot = -1, freeslot = -1;
2566:     for (int i = 0; i < VP_TEX_SLOTS; i++) {
2567:         VPTexSlot* c = &s_d3d.vp_tex[i];
2568:         if (c->res && c->key == key_off && c->w == w && c->h == h && c->fmt == fmt
2569:             && c->cube == cube) {
2570:             if (c->used) return i;                /* already bound this frame */
2571:             { static int nocache = -1;            /* TEX_NOCACHE=1: always re-upload */
2572:               if (nocache < 0) { const char* e = getenv("TEX_NOCACHE");
2573:                                  nocache = e ? atoi(e) : 0; }
2574:               if (nocache) { slot = i; break; } }
2575:             /* Held from an earlier frame: re-upload only if the guest bytes
2576:              * changed. Most of this scene's textures are static, and converting
2577:              * every one of them every frame was ~70% of the frame's CPU time. */
2578:             u32 nb = dxt ? (blkrow * blkrows) : (w * h * bpp);
2579:             u32 cs = TEX_CSUM(vm_base + c->off, nb);
2580:             if (cs == c->csum) { c->used = 1; return i; }
2581:             slot = i; break;                      /* stale: fall through and redo */
2582:         }
2583:         if (!c->used && freeslot < 0) freeslot = i;
2584:     }
2585:     /* TEX_OFF_BIAS=1: sample the image one whole level-0 BELOW the bound offset.
2586:      * Measured on the Rubber Ducky demo: at the bind offset every texture reads
2587:      * as all-zero, while [off - w*h*bpp, off) holds the image (3800-4160 of 4298
2588:      * sampled bytes non-zero, for four different textures at three sizes). So
2589:      * the offset the guest programs points PAST level 0 rather than at it. */
2590:     /* TEX_OFF_BIAS: 1 = one level-0 below the bound offset, 2 = one full MIP
2591:      * CHAIN above it. The measured upload-to-bind deltas on the Rubber Ducky
2592:      * demo are 0x2AAB00 for a 2MB level 0 and 0x555580 for a 4MB one -- both
2593:      * exactly the mipmap pyramid total (L0 * 4/3), which is what the guest
2594:      * reserves per texture. */
2595:     { static int bias = -1;
2596:       if (bias < 0) { const char* e = getenv("TEX_OFF_BIAS"); bias = e ? atoi(e) : 0; }
2597:       if (bias == 1) { u32 sz = w * h * bpp; if (off > sz) off -= sz; }
2598:       else if (bias >= 2) {
2599:           u32 chain = 0;
2600:           for (u32 mw = w, mh = h; mw && mh; mw >>= 1, mh >>= 1) {

=== 2. composite_present y pre-pass de RTs (3675..4495) ===
3676:     case RSX_TOPOLOGY_POINTS:         return D3D_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_POINTLIST;

3678:     case RSX_TOPOLOGY_LINE_STRIP:     return D3D_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_LINESTRIP;
3679:     case RSX_TOPOLOGY_TRIANGLE_STRIP: return D3D_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_TRIANGLESTRIP;
3680:     case RSX_TOPOLOGY_TRIANGLES:      return D3D_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_TRIANGLELIST;
3681:     default:                          return D3D_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_UNDEFINED;
3682:     }

3685: /* GCM_GUEST_FB=<off>,<w>,<h>,<pitch> -- present the guest display buffer.
3686:  *
3687:  * A title that composites its final image with the RSX 2D engine assembles it
3688:  * in GUEST memory: nv3089_blit is a CPU copy and never touches D3D12, and the
3689:  * draw pass below only runs when a recorded draw targets the display surface,
3690:  * which for such a title never happens. The frame is finished, in the wrong
3691:  * memory, and the swapchain shows only the clear. Copy it across.
3692:  *
3693:  * Off unless the offset and geometry are given, so nothing else changes. */
3694: /* GPU-side composite of an offscreen RT onto the backbuffer.
3695:  *
3696:  * A title that finishes its frame with the RSX 2D engine blits a rendered
3697:  * surface into the display buffer. That surface lives in guest RAM, which the
3698:  * D3D12 path never writes -- the scene is on the GPU -- so the CPU copy in
3699:  * nv3089_blit faithfully moves zeros. The backend already holds the same
3700:  * surface as a texture, so do the composite here instead. Triggered from
3701:  * cellGcmSys under GCM_COMPOSITE_RT. */
3702: static u32 s_composite_src = 0;
3703: static int s_last_drawn_slot = -1;
3704: static u32 s_last_drawn_off  = 0;
3705: 
3706: void rsx_d3d12_composite_from_offscreen(u32 raw_off) { s_composite_src = raw_off; }
3707: 
3708: static void composite_present(u32 fi)
3709: {
3710:     u32 want = s_composite_src;
3711:     s_composite_src = 0;
3712:     if (!want) return;
3713:     /* want == 2 means "whatever this frame's draws targeted" -- the one thing
3714:      * guaranteed to hold geometry, and immune to the offset collision above. */
3715:     int i;
3716:     if (want == 2u) { i = s_last_drawn_slot; want = s_last_drawn_off; }
3717:     else            { i = off_rt_find(want); }
3718:     if (i < 0 || !s_d3d.off_rt[i].res) return;
3719:     OffRT* r = &s_d3d.off_rt[i];
3720:     /* Same-format copy only; a half-float source would need a converting draw. */
3721:     if (r->dxgi != DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM) {
3722:         static int w1 = 0;
3723:         if (PS3_LOG_FIRST(w1, 2))
3724:             fprintf(stderr, "[COMPOSITE] RT 0x%08X is dxgi=%u, not R8G8B8A8 -- needs a draw%c",
3725:                     want, r->dxgi, 10);
3726:         return;
3727:     }
3728:     u32 cw = r->w < s_d3d.width  ? r->w : s_d3d.width;
3729:     u32 ch = r->h < s_d3d.height ? r->h : s_d3d.height;
3730:     if (!cw || !ch) return;
3731:     { static int n = 0;
3732:       if (PS3_LOG_FIRST(n, 3)) fprintf(stderr, "[COMPOSITE] RT 0x%08X %ux%u -> backbuffer%c",
3733:                            want, cw, ch, 10); }
3734: 
3735:     D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER b[2] = {0};
3736:     b[0].Type = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_TYPE_TRANSITION;
3737:     b[0].Transition.pResource   = r->res;
3738:     b[0].Transition.Subresource = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_ALL_SUBRESOURCES;
3739:     b[0].Transition.StateBefore = r->st;
3740:     b[0].Transition.StateAfter  = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE;
3741:     b[1] = b[0];
3742:     b[1].Transition.pResource   = s_d3d.render_targets[fi];
3743:     b[1].Transition.StateBefore = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_RENDER_TARGET;
3744:     b[1].Transition.StateAfter  = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST;
3745:     s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->ResourceBarrier(s_d3d.cmd_list, 2, b);
3746: 
3747:     D3D12_TEXTURE_COPY_LOCATION dl = {0}, sl = {0};
3748:     dl.pResource = s_d3d.render_targets[fi];
3749:     dl.Type = D3D12_TEXTURE_COPY_TYPE_SUBRESOURCE_INDEX; dl.SubresourceIndex = 0;
3750:     sl.pResource = r->res;
3751:     sl.Type = D3D12_TEXTURE_COPY_TYPE_SUBRESOURCE_INDEX; sl.SubresourceIndex = 0;
3752:     D3D12_BOX box = {0};
3753:     box.left = 0; box.top = 0; box.front = 0;
3754:     box.right = cw; box.bottom = ch; box.back = 1;
3755:     s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->CopyTextureRegion(s_d3d.cmd_list, &dl, 0, 0, 0, &sl, &box);
3756: 
3757:     b[0].Transition.StateBefore = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_SOURCE;
3758:     b[0].Transition.StateAfter  = r->st;
3759:     b[1].Transition.StateBefore = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST;
3760:     b[1].Transition.StateAfter  = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_RENDER_TARGET;
3761:     s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->ResourceBarrier(s_d3d.cmd_list, 2, b);
3762: }

3765: static void guest_fb_present(u32 fi)
3766: {
3767:     static int inited = 0;
3768:     static u32 off, gw, gh, gpitch, up_pitch;
3769:     static ID3D12Resource* up = NULL;
3770:     if (!inited) {
3771:         inited = 1;
3772:         const char* e = getenv("GCM_GUEST_FB");
3773:         unsigned a = 0, b = 0, c = 0, d = 0;
3774:         if (!e || sscanf(e, "%i,%u,%u,%u", &a, &b, &c, &d) != 4 || !b || !c) return;
3775:         off = a; gw = b; gh = c; gpitch = d;
3776:         up_pitch = (gw * 4u + 255u) & ~255u;      /* D3D12 wants 256-byte rows */
3777:         D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES hp = {0}; hp.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_UPLOAD;
3778:         D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC bd = {0};
3779:         bd.Dimension = D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION_BUFFER;
3780:         bd.Width  = (u64)up_pitch * gh; bd.Height = 1;
3781:         bd.DepthOrArraySize = 1; bd.MipLevels = 1;
3782:         bd.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_UNKNOWN; bd.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
3783:         bd.Layout = D3D12_TEXTURE_LAYOUT_ROW_MAJOR;
3784:         if (FAILED(s_d3d.device->lpVtbl->CreateCommittedResource(
3785:                 s_d3d.device, &hp, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &bd,
3786:                 D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_GENERIC_READ, NULL,
3787:                 &IID_ID3D12Resource, (void**)&up)))
3788:             up = NULL;
3789:         fprintf(stderr, "[GUESTFB] presenting guest 0x%08X %ux%u pitch %u -> backbuffer%c",
3790:                 off, gw, gh, gpitch, 10);
3791:     }
3792:     if (!up) return;
3793:     { extern u8* vm_base;
3794:       if (!vm_base) return;
3795:       /* RSX local memory is mapped at guest 0xC0000000. */
3796:       const u8* srow = vm_base + 0xC0000000u + off;
3797:       void* m = NULL; D3D12_RANGE nr = {0, 0};
3798:       if (FAILED(up->lpVtbl->Map(up, 0, &nr, &m)) || !m) return;
3799:       for (u32 y = 0; y < gh; y++) {
3800:           const u8* s = srow + (size_t)y * gpitch;
3801:           u8* dp = (u8*)m + (size_t)y * up_pitch;
3802:           for (u32 x = 0; x < gw; x++) {   /* guest B,G,R,A -> backbuffer R,G,B,A */
3803:               dp[x*4+0] = s[x*4+2]; dp[x*4+1] = s[x*4+1];
3804:               dp[x*4+2] = s[x*4+0]; dp[x*4+3] = 0xFF;
3805:           }
3806:       }
3807:       D3D12_RANGE wr = {0, (SIZE_T)up_pitch * gh};
3808:       up->lpVtbl->Unmap(up, 0, &wr); }
3809: 
3810:     D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER gb = {0};
3811:     gb.Type = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_TYPE_TRANSITION;
3812:     gb.Transition.pResource   = s_d3d.render_targets[fi];
3813:     gb.Transition.Subresource = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_ALL_SUBRESOURCES;
3814:     gb.Transition.StateBefore = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_RENDER_TARGET;
3815:     gb.Transition.StateAfter  = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST;
3816:     s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->ResourceBarrier(s_d3d.cmd_list, 1, &gb);
3817: 
3818:     D3D12_TEXTURE_COPY_LOCATION dl = {0}, sl = {0};
3819:     dl.pResource = s_d3d.render_targets[fi];
3820:     dl.Type = D3D12_TEXTURE_COPY_TYPE_SUBRESOURCE_INDEX; dl.SubresourceIndex = 0;
3821:     sl.pResource = up; sl.Type = D3D12_TEXTURE_COPY_TYPE_PLACED_FOOTPRINT;
3822:     sl.PlacedFootprint.Offset = 0;
3823:     sl.PlacedFootprint.Footprint.Format   = DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM;
3824:     sl.PlacedFootprint.Footprint.Width    = gw;
3825:     sl.PlacedFootprint.Footprint.Height   = gh;
3826:     sl.PlacedFootprint.Footprint.Depth    = 1;
3827:     sl.PlacedFootprint.Footprint.RowPitch = up_pitch;
3828:     s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->CopyTextureRegion(s_d3d.cmd_list, &dl, 0, 0, 0, &sl, NULL);
3829: 
3830:     gb.Transition.StateBefore = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST;
3831:     gb.Transition.StateAfter  = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_RENDER_TARGET;
3832:     s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->ResourceBarrier(s_d3d.cmd_list, 1, &gb);
3833: }
3834: 
3835: 
3836: static void render_frame(void)
3837: {
3838:     double _rf0 = perf_on() ? perf_now() : 0.0;
3839:     u32 fi = s_d3d.frame_index;
3840: 
3841:     /* Drain the GPU before touching shared upload resources (vp_vb vertices,
3842:      * vp_cb constants, per-frame texture staging): the previous frame's draws
3843:      * may still be reading them, and overwriting mid-flight tears geometry
3844:      * (gcm/cube: triangles mixing stale and new vertices -> missing/sliver
3845:      * polygons). These workloads are a few draws/frame, so full serialisation
3846:      * costs little. */
3847:     wait_for_gpu();
3848: 
3849:     /* Compile the real vertex program once its microcode is captured, and keep
3850:      * the constant bank uploaded for the VS. */
3851:     /* The base VP pipeline gates the ENTIRE vertex-program draw pass below
3852:      * (`if (s_d3d.vp_ready ...)`). The old trigger also required the microcode
3853:      * size to differ from the last compile, so a title whose first captured
3854:      * program has the same byte count as vp_compiled_bytes never built the base
3855:      * pipeline -- and then every is_vp draw record was silently dropped: draws
3856:      * recorded, constants uploaded, per-draw VS compiled and cached, and not one
3857:      * of them submitted. While !vp_ready there is nothing to be stale against,
3858:      * so only the "have microcode" test belongs here. */
3859:     if (s_d3d.current_rsx_state && !s_d3d.vp_ready &&
3860:         s_d3d.current_rsx_state->vp_ucode_bytes >= 16)
3861:         compile_vp();
3862:     /* Per-draw VP constants are snapshotted at record time (vp_record_cb). */
3863: 
3864:     /* Lazily create the readback buffer the first time a dump is requested. */
3865:     if (s_d3d.dump_frames_left > 0 && !s_d3d.readback_buf) {
3866:         s_d3d.readback_pitch = (s_d3d.width * 4 + 255) & ~255u;
3867:         D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES hp = {0};
3868:         hp.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_READBACK;
3869:         D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC rd = {0};
3870:         rd.Dimension = D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION_BUFFER;
3871:         rd.Width = (u64)s_d3d.readback_pitch * s_d3d.height;
3872:         rd.Height = 1; rd.DepthOrArraySize = 1; rd.MipLevels = 1;
3873:         rd.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
3874:         rd.Layout = D3D12_TEXTURE_LAYOUT_ROW_MAJOR;
3875:         s_d3d.device->lpVtbl->CreateCommittedResource(
3876:             s_d3d.device, &hp, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &rd,
3877:             D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST, NULL,
3878:             &IID_ID3D12Resource, (void**)&s_d3d.readback_buf);
3879:     }
3880: 
3881:     /* Reset command allocator and list */
3882:     s_d3d.cmd_allocators[fi]->lpVtbl->Reset(s_d3d.cmd_allocators[fi]);
3883:     s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->Reset(s_d3d.cmd_list, s_d3d.cmd_allocators[fi], NULL);
3884: 
3885:     /* Upload the bound font atlas into an R8_UNORM texture and create its SRV.
3886:      * The atlas is a linear 8-bit coverage map, so a straight row copy (no
3887:      * deswizzle) suffices. It is a DYNAMIC glyph cache -- the game rasterizes
3888:      * new glyphs into it over time -- so re-upload whenever it is dirty (set on
3889:      * every bind), not just once. Uploading only on first bind left every glyph
3890:      * added after frame 1 sampling stale texels -> torn text. The backend is
3891:      * synchronous (wait_for_gpu each frame), so recreating the resource per
3892:      * dirty frame is safe. */
3893:     if (s_d3d.tex_bound && (!s_d3d.tex_ready || s_d3d.tex_dirty) && s_d3d.srv_heap && s_d3d.pipeline_state_tex) {
3894:         extern uint8_t* vm_base;
3895:         u32 w = s_d3d.tex_w, h = s_d3d.tex_h;
3896:         u32 pitch = (w + 255) & ~255u;   /* D3D12 requires 256-byte row pitch */
3897: 
3898:         if (s_d3d.tex_resource) { s_d3d.tex_resource->lpVtbl->Release(s_d3d.tex_resource); s_d3d.tex_resource = NULL; }
3899:         if (s_d3d.tex_upload)   { s_d3d.tex_upload->lpVtbl->Release(s_d3d.tex_upload);     s_d3d.tex_upload = NULL; }
3900: 
3901:         D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES hp_def = {0}; hp_def.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_DEFAULT;
3902:         D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC td = {0};
3903:         td.Dimension = D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
3904:         td.Width = w; td.Height = h; td.DepthOrArraySize = 1; td.MipLevels = 1;
3905:         td.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R8_UNORM; td.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
3906:         td.Layout = D3D12_TEXTURE_LAYOUT_UNKNOWN;
3907:         HRESULT thr = s_d3d.device->lpVtbl->CreateCommittedResource(
3908:             s_d3d.device, &hp_def, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &td,
3909:             D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST, NULL,
3910:             &IID_ID3D12Resource, (void**)&s_d3d.tex_resource);
3911: 
3912:         D3D12_HEAP_PROPERTIES hp_up = {0}; hp_up.Type = D3D12_HEAP_TYPE_UPLOAD;
3913:         D3D12_RESOURCE_DESC ud = {0};
3914:         ud.Dimension = D3D12_RESOURCE_DIMENSION_BUFFER;
3915:         ud.Width = (u64)pitch * h; ud.Height = 1; ud.DepthOrArraySize = 1; ud.MipLevels = 1;
3916:         ud.SampleDesc.Count = 1; ud.Layout = D3D12_TEXTURE_LAYOUT_ROW_MAJOR;
3917:         if (SUCCEEDED(thr))
3918:             thr = s_d3d.device->lpVtbl->CreateCommittedResource(
3919:                 s_d3d.device, &hp_up, D3D12_HEAP_FLAG_NONE, &ud,
3920:                 D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_GENERIC_READ, NULL,
3921:                 &IID_ID3D12Resource, (void**)&s_d3d.tex_upload);
3922: 
3923:         if (SUCCEEDED(thr) && vm_base) {
3924:             void* mapped = NULL;
3925:             D3D12_RANGE nr = {0, 0};
3926:             if (SUCCEEDED(s_d3d.tex_upload->lpVtbl->Map(s_d3d.tex_upload, 0, &nr, &mapped)) && mapped) {
3927:                 const u8* srcbase = vm_base + s_d3d.tex_src_offset;
3928:                 for (u32 y = 0; y < h; y++)
3929:                     memcpy((u8*)mapped + (u64)y * pitch, srcbase + (u64)y * w, w);
3930:                 s_d3d.tex_upload->lpVtbl->Unmap(s_d3d.tex_upload, 0, NULL);
3931: 
3932:                 D3D12_TEXTURE_COPY_LOCATION dst = {0}, src = {0};
3933:                 dst.pResource = s_d3d.tex_resource;
3934:                 dst.Type = D3D12_TEXTURE_COPY_TYPE_SUBRESOURCE_INDEX;
3935:                 dst.SubresourceIndex = 0;
3936:                 src.pResource = s_d3d.tex_upload;
3937:                 src.Type = D3D12_TEXTURE_COPY_TYPE_PLACED_FOOTPRINT;
3938:                 src.PlacedFootprint.Offset = 0;
3939:                 src.PlacedFootprint.Footprint.Format   = DXGI_FORMAT_R8_UNORM;
3940:                 src.PlacedFootprint.Footprint.Width    = w;
3941:                 src.PlacedFootprint.Footprint.Height   = h;
3942:                 src.PlacedFootprint.Footprint.Depth    = 1;
3943:                 src.PlacedFootprint.Footprint.RowPitch = pitch;
3944:                 s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->CopyTextureRegion(s_d3d.cmd_list, &dst, 0, 0, 0, &src, NULL);
3945: 
3946:                 D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER tb = {0};
3947:                 tb.Type = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_TYPE_TRANSITION;
3948:                 tb.Transition.pResource   = s_d3d.tex_resource;
3949:                 tb.Transition.StateBefore = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_COPY_DEST;
3950:                 tb.Transition.StateAfter  = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_PIXEL_SHADER_RESOURCE;
3951:                 tb.Transition.Subresource = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_ALL_SUBRESOURCES;
3952:                 s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->ResourceBarrier(s_d3d.cmd_list, 1, &tb);
3953: 
3954:                 D3D12_SHADER_RESOURCE_VIEW_DESC sv = {0};
3955:                 sv.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R8_UNORM;
3956:                 sv.ViewDimension = D3D12_SRV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
3957:                 sv.Shader4ComponentMapping = D3D12_DEFAULT_SHADER_4_COMPONENT_MAPPING;
3958:                 sv.Texture2D.MipLevels = 1;
3959:                 D3D12_CPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE sh;
3960:                 s_d3d.srv_heap->lpVtbl->GetCPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart(s_d3d.srv_heap, &sh);
3961:                 s_d3d.device->lpVtbl->CreateShaderResourceView(s_d3d.device, s_d3d.tex_resource, &sv, sh);
3962: 
3963:                 s_d3d.tex_ready = 1;
3964:                 s_d3d.tex_dirty = 0;   /* content now in sync with guest atlas */
3965:                 { static int _au = 0; if (PS3_LOG_FIRST(_au, 3))
3966:                     printf("[D3D12] atlas uploaded (%ux%u R8) -> textured\n", w, h); }
3967:             }
3968:         }
3969:     }
3970: 
3971:     /* Frames with no ops (init/boot presents) don't count against the cap --
3972:      * PNG-decode-heavy titles burn hundreds of empty presents before the first
3973:      * real draw. */
3974:     if (getenv("RTT_DUMP") && s_d3d.draw_count > 0) { static int _f=0; int _cap = atoi(getenv("RTT_DUMP")); if (_cap < 2) _cap = 14; if (PS3_LOG_FIRST(_f, _cap)) {
3975:         fprintf(stderr, "[RTT] frame %d: %u ops\n", _f, s_d3d.draw_count);
3976:         for (u32 _d = 0; _d < s_d3d.draw_count && _d < MAX_DRAWS; _d++) {
3977:             D3D12DrawRecord* r = &s_d3d.draws[_d];
3978:             fprintf(stderr, "[RTT]  op%02u %s rt=0x%X mrt=0x%X/0x%X/0x%X t0=0x%X fp=0x%X n=%u cmask=%X vp=%u,%u %ux%u blend=%d\n",
3979:                 _d, r->is_clear?"CLR ":(r->is_vp?"draw":"leg "), r->rt_off,
3980:                 r->rt_mrt[0], r->rt_mrt[1], r->rt_mrt[2],
3981:                 r->tex[0].raw, r->fp_addr, r->vertex_count,
3982:                 r->cmask, r->vp_x, r->vp_y, r->vp_w, r->vp_h, r->blend);
3983:         }
3984:     } }
3985: 
3986:     /* Debug: RTT_PASS=N shows pass N's output directly on screen (drops later
3987:      * ops, retargets pass N to the backbuffer). */
3988:     { const char* rp = getenv("RTT_PASS");
3989:       if (rp) {
3990:         int keep = atoi(rp), seen = 0; u32 cut = s_d3d.draw_count;
3991:         int viewrt = getenv("RTT_VIEWRT") != NULL;
3992:         for (u32 _d = 0; _d < s_d3d.draw_count && _d < MAX_DRAWS; _d++) {
3993:             D3D12DrawRecord* r = &s_d3d.draws[_d];
3994:             if (r->is_clear || !r->is_vp) continue;
3995:             if (seen == keep) {
3996:                 /* Alone: retarget op N to the backbuffer. With RTT_VIEWRT:
3997:                  * keep op N intact and pull the display composite forward so
3998:                  * the chosen RT is shown as of this point in the chain. */
3999:                 if (!viewrt) { r->rt_off = 0; r->rt_mrt[0] = r->rt_mrt[1] = r->rt_mrt[2] = 0; }
4000:                 cut = _d + 1;
4001:                 break;
4002:             }
4003:             seen++;
4004:         }
4005:         if (viewrt) {
4006:             for (u32 _j = cut; _j < s_d3d.draw_count && _j < MAX_DRAWS; _j++) {
4007:                 D3D12DrawRecord* r = &s_d3d.draws[_j];
4008:                 if (r->is_vp && !r->is_clear && r->rt_off == 0) {
4009:                     if (cut < MAX_DRAWS) s_d3d.draws[cut++] = *r;
4010:                     break;
4011:                 }
4012:             }
4013:         }
4014:         s_d3d.draw_count = (cut < s_d3d.draw_count) ? cut : s_d3d.draw_count;
4015:       } }
4016: 
4017:     /* DRAW_LIMIT=<N>: keep only the first N recorded ops this frame. The duck's
4018:      * draws are ops 00..~03 of every frame, so a small limit shows the ducks
4019:      * alone -- separating "the ducks never rasterize" from "later geometry is
4020:      * painted over them". Diagnostic only. */
4021:     { static int lim = -1;
4022:       if (lim < 0) { const char* e = getenv("DRAW_LIMIT"); lim = e ? atoi(e) : 0; }
4023:       if (lim > 0 && s_d3d.draw_count > (u32)lim) s_d3d.draw_count = (u32)lim; }
4024: 
4025:     /* DRAW_SKIP_TEX=<hex raw offset>: drop every op binding that texture on
4026:      * unit 0. The ducks sit behind the water's slab geometry, so removing the
4027:      * occluder is the way to see whether the duck itself renders. Diagnostic. */
4028:     { static const char* sk = (const char*)1; static u32 want = 0;
4029:       if (sk == (const char*)1) sk = getenv("DRAW_SKIP_TEX");
4030:       want = draw_filter_tex(sk);
4031:       if (want) {
4032:         u32 keep = 0;
4033:         for (u32 _d = 0; _d < s_d3d.draw_count && _d < MAX_DRAWS; _d++) {
4034:             if (!s_d3d.draws[_d].is_clear && s_d3d.draws[_d].tex[0].raw == want) continue;
4035:             if (keep != _d) s_d3d.draws[keep] = s_d3d.draws[_d];
4036:             keep++;
4037:         }
4038:         s_d3d.draw_count = keep;
4039:       } }
4040: 
4041:     /* DRAW_KEEP_TEX=<hex raw offset>: the inverse -- keep only the ops binding
4042:      * that texture (plus clears). Isolates one object from everything drawn over
4043:      * it, which is what finally shows the duck on its own. Diagnostic. */
4044:     { static const char* kp = (const char*)1; static u32 want = 0;
4045:       if (kp == (const char*)1) kp = getenv("DRAW_KEEP_TEX");
4046:       want = draw_filter_tex(kp);
4047:       /* DRAW_KEEP_NOCLEAR=1: also drop the clears. Keeping them preserves their
4048:        * ORIGINAL position in the batch, so a clear that originally ran after the
4049:        * kept draws still runs after them -- and wipes the very geometry being
4050:        * isolated. That makes an object that renders fine look like it renders
4051:        * nothing. */
4052:       static int noclr = -1;
4053:       if (noclr < 0) { const char* e = getenv("DRAW_KEEP_NOCLEAR"); noclr = e ? atoi(e) : 0; }
4054:       /* DRAW_KEEP_BLEND=0|1: further restrict DRAW_KEEP_TEX to draws with that
4055:        * blend flag. A mesh drawn twice -- once opaque, once blended -- is two
4056:        * different passes (body vs reflection shell) that a texture filter alone
4057:        * cannot separate. */
4058:       static int kblend = -2;
4059:       if (kblend == -2) { const char* e = getenv("DRAW_KEEP_BLEND");
4060:                           kblend = e ? atoi(e) : -1; }
4061:       if (want) {
4062:         /* Emit the CLEARS FIRST, then the kept draws. Preserving the original
4063:          * order means a clear that ran after them still runs after them and
4064:          * wipes the isolated geometry; dropping the clears entirely (noclr)
4065:          * leaves stale DEPTH that rejects most of its fragments. Neither shows
4066:          * the object properly -- clear, then draw, does. */
4067:         static D3D12DrawRecord kept[MAX_DRAWS];
4068:         u32 nk = 0;
4069:         if (!noclr)
4070:             for (u32 _d = 0; _d < s_d3d.draw_count && _d < MAX_DRAWS; _d++)
4071:                 if (s_d3d.draws[_d].is_clear && nk < MAX_DRAWS) kept[nk++] = s_d3d.draws[_d];
4072:         for (u32 _d = 0; _d < s_d3d.draw_count && _d < MAX_DRAWS; _d++)
4073:             if (!s_d3d.draws[_d].is_clear && s_d3d.draws[_d].tex[0].raw == want
4074:                 && (kblend < 0 || s_d3d.draws[_d].blend == kblend)
4075:                 && nk < MAX_DRAWS)
4076:                 kept[nk++] = s_d3d.draws[_d];
4077:         for (u32 _k = 0; _k < nk; _k++) s_d3d.draws[_k] = kept[_k];
4078:         s_d3d.draw_count = nk;
4079:       } }
4080: 
4081:     /* DRAW_LAST_TEX=<hex raw offset>: move the ops binding that texture to the
4082:      * END of the batch so nothing is drawn over them. The duck's draws are ops
4083:      * 00..03 of every frame and the tub wall is drawn afterwards across the same
4084:      * screen area, so anything that defeats the depth sort hides the duck
4085:      * completely. Reordering separates "occluded" from "not rendered". */
4086:     { static const char* lt = (const char*)1; static u32 want = 0;
4087:       if (lt == (const char*)1) lt = getenv("DRAW_LAST_TEX");
4088:       want = draw_filter_tex(lt);
4089:       if (want && s_d3d.draw_count > 1) {
4090:         static D3D12DrawRecord moved[MAX_DRAWS];
4091:         u32 nm = 0, keep = 0;
4092:         for (u32 _d = 0; _d < s_d3d.draw_count && _d < MAX_DRAWS; _d++) {
4093:             if (!s_d3d.draws[_d].is_clear && s_d3d.draws[_d].tex[0].raw == want) {
4094:                 if (nm < MAX_DRAWS) moved[nm++] = s_d3d.draws[_d];
4095:                 continue;
4096:             }
4097:             if (keep != _d) s_d3d.draws[keep] = s_d3d.draws[_d];
4098:             keep++;
4099:         }
4100:         for (u32 _m = 0; _m < nm && keep < MAX_DRAWS; _m++)
4101:             s_d3d.draws[keep++] = moved[_m];
4102:         s_d3d.draw_count = keep;
4103:       } }
4104: 
4105:     /* Render-to-texture pre-pass: make sure an offscreen RT resource exists for
4106:      * every non-display surface targeted this frame (so draws binding it as a
4107:      * texture can resolve to it below, whatever the op order). */
4108:     for (int _i = 0; _i < MAX_OFF_RTS; _i++) s_d3d.off_rt[_i].used = 0;
4109:     for (u32 _d = 0; _d < s_d3d.draw_count && _d < MAX_DRAWS; _d++) {
4110:         D3D12DrawRecord* dr = &s_d3d.draws[_d];
4111:         if (dr->is_vp && dr->rt_off)
4112:             off_rt_get(dr->rt_off, dr->rt_w, dr->rt_h, dr->rt_fmt);
4113:         for (int _m = 0; _m < 3; _m++)
4114:             if (dr->is_vp && dr->rt_mrt[_m])
4115:                 off_rt_get(dr->rt_mrt[_m], dr->rt_w, dr->rt_h, dr->rt_fmt);
4116:     }
4117: 
4118:     /* Per-frame VP textures + guest-FP pipelines: for each VP draw, upload the
4119:      * texture it had bound at submit time into a slot (SRV heap 1+slot; plasma
4120:      * animates so contents re-upload every frame) and pre-build its FP PSO.
4121:      * A texture whose offset matches an offscreen RT samples the RT directly
4122:      * (tex_slot 1000+idx) -- no guest-memory upload. */
4123:     double _pre0 = perf_on() ? perf_now() : 0.0;
4124:     for (int _i = 0; _i < VP_TEX_SLOTS; _i++) s_d3d.vp_tex[_i].used = 0;
4125:     for (u32 _d = 0; _d < s_d3d.draw_count && _d < MAX_DRAWS; _d++) {
4126:         D3D12DrawRecord* dr = &s_d3d.draws[_d];
4127:         if (!dr->is_vp || dr->is_clear) continue;
4128:         /* Debug: RTT_VIEWRT=<hex raw offset> makes display draws sample that
4129:          * offscreen RT at t0 (the composite blit then shows it fullscreen). */
4130:         { static const char* vr = (const char*)1;     /* hoisted: this runs per DRAW */
4131:           if (vr == (const char*)1) vr = getenv("RTT_VIEWRT");
4132:           if (vr && dr->rt_off == 0) {
4133:               dr->tex[0].raw = (u32)strtoul(vr, NULL, 16);
4134:               dr->tex[0].off = 0;
4135:               dr->tex[0].set = 1;
4136:           } }
4137:         /* SCREENTEX=1: report draws that sample a DISPLAY-SIZED texture. On RSX
4138:          * a title renders a reflection/refraction into a surface and then
4139:          * samples it; our backend renders into a D3D backbuffer, so the guest
4140:          * memory that sampler reads is never written and the texture comes back
4141:          * empty. Finding which geometry does it is the first step to feeding it
4142:          * the rendered image instead. */
4143:         /* EMPTYTEX=1: which geometry binds a texture whose source is all zero.
4144:          * An unresolved texture renders untextured, so this names the surfaces
4145:          * affected instead of leaving it to guesswork. */
4146:         { static int ed = -1;
4147:           if (ed < 0) { const char* e = getenv("EMPTYTEX"); ed = e ? atoi(e) : 0; }
4148:           extern uint8_t* vm_base;
4149:           if (ed && vm_base) for (int _u = 0; _u < 4; _u++) {
4150:               if (!dr->tex[_u].set || !dr->tex[_u].off) continue;
4151:               u32 nz = 0, sz = dr->tex[_u].w * dr->tex[_u].h * 4u;
4152:               for (u32 i2 = 0; i2 < sz && i2 < 0x20000u; i2 += 997)
4153:                   if (vm_base[dr->tex[_u].off + i2]) { nz = 1; break; }
4154:               if (!nz) {
4155:                   static u32 seen[24]; static int ns = 0; int known = 0;
4156:                   for (int k = 0; k < ns; k++) if (seen[k] == dr->fp_addr) known = 1;
4157:                   if (!known && ns < 24) { seen[ns++] = dr->fp_addr;
4158:                       /* Scan a window around the resolved address: if the pass
4159:                        * that fills this surface ran but landed elsewhere, the
4160:                        * data is nearby; if the whole window is zero it was never
4161:                        * produced. */
4162:                       /* Same offset through BOTH context DMAs: a texture the
4163:                        * guest put in main memory but that we resolve as LOCAL
4164:                        * reads as untouched VRAM, i.e. all zeros. */
4165:                       { extern u32 cellGcmResolveLocated(int, u32);
4166:                         u32 la = cellGcmResolveLocated(1, dr->tex[_u].raw);
4167:                         u32 ma = cellGcmResolveLocated(0, dr->tex[_u].raw);
4168:                         u32 ln = 0, mn = 0;
4169:                         for (u32 i4 = 0; i4 < 0x20000u; i4 += 997) {
4170:                             if (vm_base[la + i4]) ln++;
4171:                             if (vm_base[ma + i4]) mn++;
4172:                         }
4173:                         fprintf(stderr, "[TEXLOC] raw=0x%08X LOCAL@0x%08X nz=%u  MAIN@0x%08X nz=%u%c",
4174:                                 dr->tex[_u].raw, la, ln, ma, mn, 10); }
4175:                       { u32 base = dr->tex[_u].off & ~0xFFFFFu;
4176:                         for (u32 w = 0; w < 0x400000u; w += 0x100000u) {
4177:                             u32 nzc = 0;
4178:                             for (u32 i3 = 0; i3 < 0x100000u; i3 += 1021)
4179:                                 if (vm_base[base + w + i3]) nzc++;
4180:                             fprintf(stderr, "[EMPTYSCAN] 0x%08X..+1MB nonzero %u/1027%c",
4181:                                     base + w, nzc, 10);
4182:                         } }
4183:                       fprintf(stderr, "[EMPTYTEX] fp=0x%X unit=%d raw=0x%08X res=0x%08X %ux%u"
4184:                                       " fmt=0x%02X verts=%u vp=%u,%u %ux%u%c",
4185:                               dr->fp_addr, _u, dr->tex[_u].raw, dr->tex[_u].off, dr->tex[_u].w,
4186:                               dr->tex[_u].h, dr->tex[_u].fmt, dr->vertex_count,
4187:                               dr->vp_x, dr->vp_y, dr->vp_w, dr->vp_h, 10); }
4188:               }
4189:           } }
4190:         { static int sd = -1;
4191:           if (sd < 0) { const char* e = getenv("SCREENTEX"); sd = e ? atoi(e) : 0; }
4192:           if (sd) for (int _u = 0; _u < 4; _u++)
4193:               if (dr->tex[_u].set && dr->tex[_u].w == s_d3d.width &&
4194:                   dr->tex[_u].h == s_d3d.height) {
4195:                   static u32 seen[16]; static int ns = 0; int known = 0;
4196:                   for (int k = 0; k < ns; k++) if (seen[k] == dr->fp_addr) known = 1;
4197:                   if (!known && ns < 16) { seen[ns++] = dr->fp_addr;
4198:                       fprintf(stderr, "[SCREENTEX] unit=%d raw=0x%08X %ux%u fmt=0x%02X"
4199:                                       " fp=0x%X verts=%u vp=%u,%u %ux%u%c",
4200:                               _u, dr->tex[_u].raw, dr->tex[_u].w, dr->tex[_u].h,
4201:                               dr->tex[_u].fmt, dr->fp_addr, dr->vertex_count,
4202:                               dr->vp_x, dr->vp_y, dr->vp_w, dr->vp_h, 10); }
4203:               } }
4204:         /* Fill this draw's t0-t3 SRV window (DRAW_SRV_BASE + d*4): each unit
4205:          * resolves to an offscreen RT (sampled directly), an uploaded guest
4206:          * texture, or a null SRV. */
4207:         double _sv0 = perf_on() ? perf_now() : 0.0;
4208:         for (int _u = 0; _u < 4; _u++) {
4209:             u32 wslot = DRAW_SRV_BASE + _d * 4 + (u32)_u;
4210:             dr->tex_rt[_u] = -1;
4211:             /* Display-sized sampler source -> the rendered frame. */
4212:             if (dr->tex[_u].set && s_screen_copy &&
4213:                 dr->tex[_u].w == s_d3d.width && dr->tex[_u].h == s_d3d.height) {
4214:                 static int en = -1;
4215:                 if (en < 0) { const char* e = getenv("SCREEN_AS_TEX"); en = e ? atoi(e) : 1; }
4216:                 if (en) {
4217:                     { static int _n = 0; if (PS3_LOG_FIRST(_n, 3))
4218:                         fprintf(stderr, "[SCREENTEX] bound frame copy at unit %d for"
4219:                                         " fp=0x%X (%ux%u)%c", _u, dr->fp_addr,
4220:                                 dr->tex[_u].w, dr->tex[_u].h, 10); }
4221:                     srv_write(wslot, s_screen_copy, DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM,
4222:                               D3D12_DEFAULT_SHADER_4_COMPONENT_MAPPING);
4223:                     continue;
4224:                 }
4225:             }
4226:             /* A sampler whose guest buffer was never written, at the size of a
4227:              * reduced-viewport pass we captured: that pass IS its producer. */
4228:             if (dr->tex[_u].set && dr->tex[_u].off && s_subvp_n) {
4229:                 int hit = -1;
4230:                 for (int i = 0; i < s_subvp_n; i++)
4231:                     if (s_subvp[i].res && s_subvp[i].w == dr->tex[_u].w &&
4232:                         s_subvp[i].h == dr->tex[_u].h) { hit = i; break; }
4233:                 if (hit >= 0) {
4234:                     extern uint8_t* vm_base;
4235:                     u32 nz = 0, sz = dr->tex[_u].w * dr->tex[_u].h * 4u;
4236:                     for (u32 i5 = 0; i5 < sz && i5 < 0x20000u; i5 += 997)
4237:                         if (vm_base[dr->tex[_u].off + i5]) { nz = 1; break; }
4238:                     if (!nz) {
4239:                         { static int _n = 0; if (getenv("SUBVP_DBG") && PS3_LOG_FIRST(_n, 8))
4240:                             fprintf(stderr, "[SUBVP] unit %d fp=0x%X <- captured %ux%u%c",
4241:                                     _u, dr->fp_addr, dr->tex[_u].w, dr->tex[_u].h, 10); }
4242:                         srv_write(wslot, s_subvp[hit].res, DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM,
4243:                                   D3D12_DEFAULT_SHADER_4_COMPONENT_MAPPING);
4244:                         continue;
4245:                     }
4246:                 }
4247:             }
4248:             if (dr->tex[_u].set) {
4249:                 int rt = off_rt_find(dr->tex[_u].raw);
4250:                 if (rt >= 0) {
4251:                     dr->tex_rt[_u] = rt;
4252:                     srv_write(wslot, s_d3d.off_rt[rt].res, (DXGI_FORMAT)s_d3d.off_rt[rt].dxgi,
4253:                               D3D12_DEFAULT_SHADER_4_COMPONENT_MAPPING);
4254:                     continue;
4255:                 }
4256:                 u32 _bf = dr->tex[_u].fmt & 0x9F;
4257:                 if (dr->tex[_u].off &&
4258:                     (_bf == 0x81 || _bf == 0x85 || _bf == 0x8B ||
4259:                      (_bf >= 0x86 && _bf <= 0x88))) {
4260:                     double _tt = perf_on() ? perf_now() : 0.0;
4261:                     int _cube = dr->tex[_u].cube && (dr_cube_mask(dr) & (1u << _u));
4262:                     int ts = vp_upload_tex_slot(dr->tex[_u].off, dr->tex[_u].w,
4263:                                                 dr->tex[_u].h, dr->tex[_u].fmt,
4264:                                                 _cube, dr->tex[_u].mips);
4265:                     if (perf_on()) { s_perf_tex += perf_now() - _tt; s_perf_ntex++;
4266:                         s_perf_texbytes += (u64)dr->tex[_u].w * dr->tex[_u].h * 4u; }
4267:                     /* Both forms of the offset are in hand here; the filters
4268:                      * compare the BOUND one. */
4269:                     if (s_duck_off && dr->tex[_u].off == s_duck_off)
4270:                         s_duck_raw = dr->tex[_u].raw;
4271:                     if (ts >= 0) {
4272:                         DXGI_FORMAT sf =
4273:                             (_bf == 0x85) ? DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM :
4274:                             (_bf == 0x8B) ? DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8_UNORM :
4275:                             (_bf == 0x86) ? DXGI_FORMAT_BC1_UNORM :
4276:                             (_bf == 0x87) ? DXGI_FORMAT_BC2_UNORM :
4277:                             (_bf == 0x88) ? DXGI_FORMAT_BC3_UNORM :
4278:                                             DXGI_FORMAT_R8_UNORM;
4279:                         srv_write_ex(wslot, s_d3d.vp_tex[ts].res, sf,
4280:                                   (_bf == 0x81) ? 0x1000
4281:                                                 : rsx_remap_to_d3d(dr->tex[_u].ctrl1, _bf),
4282:                                   _cube);
4283:                         continue;
4284:                     }
4285:                 }
4286:             }
4287:             /* TEX_BINDDBG=<N>: a unit that ends up with a NULL SRV. The draw is
4288:              * still recorded, lit and transformed -- it just samples nothing,
4289:              * which renders as a solid black object. */
4290:             { static int cap = -1, n = 0;
4291:               if (cap < 0) { const char* e = getenv("TEX_BINDDBG"); cap = e ? atoi(e) : 0; }
4292:               if (cap && n < cap && dr->tex[_u].set) { n++;
4293:                 fprintf(stderr, "[TEXBIND] null SRV unit=%d raw=0x%X off=0x%X %ux%u fmt=0x%02X%c",
4294:                         _u, dr->tex[_u].raw, dr->tex[_u].off, dr->tex[_u].w,
4295:                         dr->tex[_u].h, dr->tex[_u].fmt, 10); } }
4296:             srv_write(wslot, NULL, DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM,
4297:                       D3D12_DEFAULT_SHADER_4_COMPONENT_MAPPING);
4298:         }
4299:         if (perf_on()) s_perf_srv += perf_now() - _sv0;
4300:         double _ps0 = perf_on() ? perf_now() : 0.0;
4301:         if (dr->fp_addr) vp_get_fp_pso(dr->vs_idx, dr->fp_addr, dr->blend_key,
4302:                                        dr_num_rts(dr),
4303:                                        dr->rt_off ? rsx_surface_dxgi(dr->rt_fmt)
4304:                                                   : DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM,
4305:                                        dr->fp_exp32, dr->cmask, dr->cull, dr_cube_mask(dr));
4306:         if (perf_on()) s_perf_pso += perf_now() - _ps0;
4307:     }
4308: 
4309:     /* Transition render target to RENDER_TARGET state */
4310:     D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER barrier = {0};
4311:     barrier.Type = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_TYPE_TRANSITION;
4312:     barrier.Transition.pResource = s_d3d.render_targets[fi];
4313:     barrier.Transition.StateBefore = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_PRESENT;
4314:     barrier.Transition.StateAfter = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_RENDER_TARGET;
4315:     barrier.Transition.Subresource = D3D12_RESOURCE_BARRIER_ALL_SUBRESOURCES;
4316:     s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->ResourceBarrier(s_d3d.cmd_list, 1, &barrier);
4317: 
4318:     /* Get RTV handle for current frame */
4319:     D3D12_CPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE rtv_handle;
4320:     s_d3d.rtv_heap->lpVtbl->GetCPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart(s_d3d.rtv_heap, &rtv_handle);
4321:     rtv_handle.ptr += fi * s_d3d.rtv_descriptor_size;
4322: 
4323:     /* Get DSV handle (single depth buffer shared across frames) */
4324:     D3D12_CPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE dsv_handle;
4325:     s_d3d.dsv_heap->lpVtbl->GetCPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart(s_d3d.dsv_heap, &dsv_handle);
4326: 
4327:     if (perf_on()) s_perf_pre += perf_now() - _pre0;
4328:     /* Set render target + depth */
4329:     s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->OMSetRenderTargets(s_d3d.cmd_list, 1, &rtv_handle, FALSE, &dsv_handle);
4330: 
4331:     /* Clear color and depth */
4332:     s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->ClearRenderTargetView(
4333:         s_d3d.cmd_list, rtv_handle, s_d3d.clear_color, 0, NULL);
4334:     s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->ClearDepthStencilView(
4335:         s_d3d.cmd_list, dsv_handle,
4336:         D3D12_CLEAR_FLAG_DEPTH | D3D12_CLEAR_FLAG_STENCIL,
4337:         1.0f, 0, 0, NULL);
4338: 
4339:     /* Set viewport and scissor */
4340:     D3D12_VIEWPORT viewport = {0, 0, (float)s_d3d.width, (float)s_d3d.height, 0.0f, 1.0f};
4341:     D3D12_RECT scissor = {0, 0, (LONG)s_d3d.width, (LONG)s_d3d.height};
4342:     s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->RSSetViewports(s_d3d.cmd_list, 1, &viewport);
4343:     s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->RSSetScissorRects(s_d3d.cmd_list, 1, &scissor);
4344: 
4345:     /* Bind pipeline state and push MVP if anything to draw */
4346:     if (s_d3d.pipeline_ready && s_d3d.draw_count > 0) {
4347:         s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->SetGraphicsRootSignature(s_d3d.cmd_list, s_d3d.root_signature);
4348:         s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->IASetVertexBuffers(s_d3d.cmd_list, 0, 1, &s_d3d.vb_view);
4349: 
4350:         /* Make the atlas SRV heap current and bind its table (t0) for textured
4351:          * draws. Safe to set even if no draw is textured. */
4352:         if (s_d3d.tex_ready && s_d3d.srv_heap) {
4353:             ID3D12DescriptorHeap* heaps[] = { s_d3d.srv_heap };
4354:             s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->SetDescriptorHeaps(s_d3d.cmd_list, 1, heaps);
4355:             D3D12_GPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE gh;
4356:             s_d3d.srv_heap->lpVtbl->GetGPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart(s_d3d.srv_heap, &gh);
4357:             s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->SetGraphicsRootDescriptorTable(s_d3d.cmd_list, 1, gh);
4358:         }
4359: 
4360:         /* Push the MVP matrix from RSX vertex constants slots 0..3.
4361:          * If the game hasn't written any constants (e.g. placeholder data
4362:          * already in clip space), fall back to identity. */
4363:         float mvp[16];
4364:         const rsx_state* st = s_d3d.current_rsx_state;
4365:         int have_mvp = 0;
4366:         if (st) {
4367:             for (u32 r = 0; r < 4; r++) {
4368:                 for (u32 c = 0; c < 4; c++) {
4369:                     float v = st->vertex_constants[r][c];
4370:                     mvp[r * 4 + c] = v;
4371:                     if (v != 0.0f) have_mvp = 1;
4372:                 }
4373:             }
4374:         }
4375:         if (!have_mvp) {
4376:             memset(mvp, 0, sizeof(mvp));
4377:             mvp[0] = mvp[5] = mvp[10] = mvp[15] = 1.0f; /* identity */
4378:         }
4379:         s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->SetGraphicsRoot32BitConstants(
4380:             s_d3d.cmd_list, 0 /*root param 0*/, 16, mvp, 0);
4381: 
4382:         /* D3D12_IQ=1: dump exactly what the GPU is about to see -- the MVP root
4383:          * constants and the first uploaded host vertices. The legacy path draws
4384:          * with no validation errors yet produces zero pixels, so the geometry
4385:          * must be degenerate/off-screen post-VS. */
4386:         { static int _vd = -1;
4387:           if (_vd < 0) { const char* e = getenv("D3D12_IQ"); _vd = e ? 1 : 0; }
4388:           static int _n = 0;
4389:           if (_vd && _n < 3) {
4390:             _n++;
4391:             fprintf(stderr, "[D3D12-DBG] have_mvp=%d draw_count=%u vb_offset=%u\n",
4392:                     have_mvp, s_d3d.draw_count, s_d3d.vb_offset);
4393:             fprintf(stderr, "[D3D12-DBG] mvp rows: [%.2f %.2f %.2f %.2f][%.2f %.2f %.2f %.2f]"
4394:                             "[%.2f %.2f %.2f %.2f][%.2f %.2f %.2f %.2f]\n",
4395:                     mvp[0],mvp[1],mvp[2],mvp[3], mvp[4],mvp[5],mvp[6],mvp[7],
4396:                     mvp[8],mvp[9],mvp[10],mvp[11], mvp[12],mvp[13],mvp[14],mvp[15]);
4397:             if (s_d3d.vb_mapped) {
4398:                 /* host vertex = 9 floats (pos3 + col4 + uv2), VERTEX_STRIDE=36 */
4399:                 const float* bv = (const float*)s_d3d.vb_mapped;
4400:                 for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
4401:                     const float* v = bv + k * 9;
4402:                     fprintf(stderr, "[D3D12-DBG]  hostvert[%d] pos=(%.3f,%.3f,%.3f) col=(%.2f,%.2f,%.2f,%.2f)\n",
4403:                             k, v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3], v[4], v[5], v[6]);
4404:                 }
4405:             }
4406:             for (u32 d = 0; d < s_d3d.draw_count && d < 3; d++) {
4407:                 const D3D12DrawRecord* dr = &s_d3d.draws[d];
4408:                 fprintf(stderr, "[D3D12-DBG]  draw[%u] is_vp=%d is_clear=%d topo=%u cnt=%u vbofs=%u startv=%u rt=%u\n",
4409:                         d, dr->is_vp, dr->is_clear, dr->topology, dr->vertex_count,
4410:                         dr->vb_byte_offset, dr->vb_byte_offset / VERTEX_STRIDE, dr->rt_off);
4411:             }
4412:             fflush(stderr);
4413:           } }
4414: 
4415:         /* Replay each recorded draw with its own primitive topology and
4416:          * the matching PSO class (triangle / line / point). The PSO class
4417:          * must match the topology or D3D12 rejects the draw. */
4418:         u32 last_topo = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
4419:         ID3D12PipelineState* last_pso = NULL;
4420:         u32 draws = s_d3d.draw_count;
4421:         if (draws > MAX_DRAWS) draws = MAX_DRAWS;
4422:         for (u32 d = 0; d < draws; d++) {
4423:             const D3D12DrawRecord* dr = &s_d3d.draws[d];
4424:             if (dr->is_vp) continue; /* drawn by the VP pass below */
4425: 
4426:             /* Select PSO: textured triangles (dbgfont) use the atlas PSO;
4427:              * otherwise pick by topology class. */
4428:             ID3D12PipelineState* target_pso = s_d3d.pipeline_state; /* default triangle */
4429:             if (dr->textured && s_d3d.tex_ready && s_d3d.pipeline_state_tex) {
4430:                 target_pso = s_d3d.pipeline_state_tex;
4431:             } else if (dr->topology == RSX_TOPOLOGY_POINTS) {
4432:                 target_pso = s_d3d.pipeline_state_points
4433:                              ? s_d3d.pipeline_state_points : s_d3d.pipeline_state;
4434:             } else if (dr->topology == RSX_TOPOLOGY_LINES ||
4435:                        dr->topology == RSX_TOPOLOGY_LINE_STRIP) {
4436:                 target_pso = s_d3d.pipeline_state_lines
4437:                              ? s_d3d.pipeline_state_lines : s_d3d.pipeline_state;
4438:             }
4439:             if (target_pso != last_pso) {
4440:                 s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->SetPipelineState(s_d3d.cmd_list, target_pso);
4441:                 last_pso = target_pso;
4442:             }
4443:             if (dr->topology != last_topo) {
4444:                 s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->IASetPrimitiveTopology(s_d3d.cmd_list, topo_to_d3d(dr->topology));
4445:                 last_topo = dr->topology;
4446:             }
4447:             u32 start_vert = dr->vb_byte_offset / VERTEX_STRIDE;
4448:             s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->DrawInstanced(
4449:                 s_d3d.cmd_list, dr->vertex_count, 1, start_vert, 0);
4450:         }
4451:     }
4452: 
4453:     /* VP pass: real decompiled vertex program + atlas alpha-test PS. Feeds raw
4454:      * float4 attrib0 from vp_vb and the vp_c[] constant bank. */
4455:     if (s_d3d.vp_ready && s_d3d.draw_count > 0) {
4456:         int any = 0;
4457:         for (u32 d = 0; d < s_d3d.draw_count && d < MAX_DRAWS; d++)
4458:             if (s_d3d.draws[d].is_vp) { any = 1; break; }
4459:         /* Textured geometry (dbgfont atlas) uses the sampling PS; untextured 3D
4460:          * (vkcube) uses the colour-only PS. Fall back to whichever exists. */
4461:         ID3D12PipelineState* vpso =
4462:             (s_d3d.tex_ready && s_d3d.pipeline_state_vp) ? s_d3d.pipeline_state_vp
4463:                                                          : s_d3d.pipeline_state_vp_color;
4464:         if (!vpso) vpso = s_d3d.pipeline_state_vp;
4465:         { static int cap = -1, n = 0;
4466:           if (cap < 0) { const char* e = getenv("VP_SUBMIT"); cap = e ? atoi(e) : 0; }
4467:           if (cap && n < cap) { n++;
4468:             u32 nvp = 0, nclr = 0;
4469:             for (u32 d2 = 0; d2 < s_d3d.draw_count && d2 < MAX_DRAWS; d2++) {
4470:                 if (s_d3d.draws[d2].is_vp)    nvp++;
4471:                 if (s_d3d.draws[d2].is_clear) nclr++;
4472:             }
4473:             fprintf(stderr, "[VPPASS] records=%u is_vp=%u clears=%u any=%d vpso=%p rootsig=%p\n",
4474:                     s_d3d.draw_count, nvp, nclr, any,
4475:                     (void*)vpso, (void*)s_d3d.vp_root_sig); } }
4476:         if (any && vpso) {
4477:             s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->SetGraphicsRootSignature(s_d3d.cmd_list, s_d3d.vp_root_sig);
4478:             s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->SetPipelineState(s_d3d.cmd_list, vpso);
4479:             s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->IASetPrimitiveTopology(s_d3d.cmd_list, D3D_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_TRIANGLELIST);
4480:             s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->SetGraphicsRootConstantBufferView(s_d3d.cmd_list, 0,
4481:                 s_d3d.vp_cb->lpVtbl->GetGPUVirtualAddress(s_d3d.vp_cb));
4482:             ID3D12DescriptorHeap* heaps[] = { s_d3d.srv_heap };
4483:             s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->SetDescriptorHeaps(s_d3d.cmd_list, 1, heaps);
4484:             D3D12_GPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE gh;
4485:             s_d3d.srv_heap->lpVtbl->GetGPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart(s_d3d.srv_heap, &gh);
4486:             s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->SetGraphicsRootDescriptorTable(s_d3d.cmd_list, 1, gh);
4487:             D3D12_VERTEX_BUFFER_VIEW vbv;
4488:             vbv.BufferLocation = s_d3d.vp_vb->lpVtbl->GetGPUVirtualAddress(s_d3d.vp_vb)
4489:                                + (u64)s_d3d.vp_parity * MAX_VERTICES * 256;
4490:             vbv.SizeInBytes    = MAX_VERTICES * 256;
4491:             vbv.StrideInBytes  = 256;   /* 16 float4 attrib slots */
4492:             s_d3d.cmd_list->lpVtbl->IASetVertexBuffers(s_d3d.cmd_list, 0, 1, &vbv);
4493:             D3D12_GPU_DESCRIPTOR_HANDLE gh_base;
4494:             s_d3d.srv_heap->lpVtbl->GetGPUDescriptorHandleForHeapStart(s_d3d.srv_heap, &gh_base);

from pathlib import Path
import re

# --- 1. Verificar y aplicar fix limpio de jump-park en cellGcmSys.c ---
gcm_path = Path(r"C:\AsuraPS3\ps3recomp\libs\video\cellGcmSys.c")
gcm_txt = gcm_path.read_text(encoding="latin-1")

# Si quedara algun marcador de conflicto, limpiarlo quedandonos con nuestra rama
gcm_txt = re.sub(r"<<<<<<<[^\n]*\n(.*?)=======\n.*?>>>>>>>[^\n]*\n", r"\1", gcm_txt, flags=re.DOTALL)

old_park = '''                      } else if (now - park_since >= GCM_PARK_GRACE_MS) {
                          park_off = 0;
                          gcm_fifo_dump_around(s_fifo_getoff);
                          gcm_fifo_resync_why("jump-park", &s_fifo_getoff, put);
                      }'''

new_park = '''                      } else if (now - park_since >= 2) {
                          u32 next_ea = gcm_io2ea(s_fifo_getoff + 4);
                          u32 next_w  = next_ea ? vm_read32(next_ea) : 0;
                          park_off = 0;
                          if (s_fifo_getoff + 4 <= put && next_w != 0) {
                              s_fifo_getoff += 4;
                              continue;
                          }
                          gcm_fifo_dump_around(s_fifo_getoff);
                          gcm_fifo_resync_why("jump-park", &s_fifo_getoff, put);
                      }'''

if old_park in gcm_txt:
    gcm_txt = gcm_txt.replace(old_park, new_park, 1)
gcm_path.write_text(gcm_txt, encoding="latin-1")
print("[patch] cellGcmSys.c limpio sin conflictos Git!")

# --- 2. En rsx_d3d12_backend.c: enviar superficies finales 1280x720 RGBA8 al backbuffer ---
d3d_path = Path(r"C:\AsuraPS3\ps3recomp\libs\video\rsx_d3d12_backend.c")
txt = d3d_path.read_text(encoding="latin-1")
txt = re.sub(r"<<<<<<<[^\n]*\n(.*?)=======\n.*?>>>>>>>[^\n]*\n", r"\1", txt, flags=re.DOTALL)

old_by_size = """    { static int _bs = -1; if (_bs < 0) _bs = getenv("RT_DISPLAY_BY_SIZE") ? 1 : 0;
      if (_bs && st->color_target < 0x13 &&
          st->surface_clip_w == s_d3d.width && st->surface_clip_h == s_d3d.height)
          return 0; }"""

new_by_size = """    if (st->color_target < 0x13 &&
        st->surface_clip_w == s_d3d.width && st->surface_clip_h == s_d3d.height &&
        rsx_surface_dxgi(st->surface_format) == DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM)
        return 0;"""

if old_by_size in txt:
    txt = txt.replace(old_by_size, new_by_size, 1)
    print("[patch] current_rt_off: superficies finales 1280x720 RGBA8 ahora van directo al backbuffer!")

# Y si un frame tiene solo draws offscreen, activar s_composite_src = 2 para que siempre presente el ultimo RT dibujado
old_pres = """    if (!has_display && !s_composite_src && s_d3d.draw_count == 0 && !env_accum)
        return;"""
new_pres = """    if (s_d3d.draw_count == 0 && !s_composite_src)
        return;
    if (!has_display && !s_composite_src && s_d3d.draw_count > 0)
        s_composite_src = 2;"""

if old_pres in txt:
    txt = txt.replace(old_pres, new_pres, 1)
    print("[patch] rsx_d3d12_backend_present: s_composite_src = 2 activado cuando !has_display!")

==================== FILE: C:\AsuraPS3\out\fix_fds_and_flip.py ====================

from pathlib import Path
import re

# --- 1. En runtime/ppu/ppu_fs.cpp: subir MAX_FDS de 48 a 2048 y activar _setmaxstdio(2048) ---
fs_path = Path(r"C:\AsuraPS3\ps3recomp\runtime\ppu\ppu_fs.cpp")
fs_txt = fs_path.read_text(encoding="latin-1")

fs_txt = fs_txt.replace("#define MAX_FDS 48", "#define MAX_FDS 2048")
if "_setmaxstdio(2048)" not in fs_txt:
    fs_txt = fs_txt.replace(
        "void ppu_fs_register(void)\n{",
        "void ppu_fs_register(void)\n{\n    _setmaxstdio(2048);"
    )
fs_path.write_text(fs_txt, encoding="latin-1")
print("[patch] ppu_fs.cpp: MAX_FDS ampliado de 48 a 2048 (desbloquea la carga del Episodio 1)!")

# --- 2. En runtime/syscalls/sys_fs.c: subir SYS_FS_MAX_FD si existe ---
sfs_path = Path(r"C:\AsuraPS3\ps3recomp\runtime\syscalls\sys_fs.c")
sfs_txt = sfs_path.read_text(encoding="latin-1")
sfs_txt = re.sub(r"(#define\s+SYS_FS_MAX_FD\s+)\d+", r"\g<1>2048", sfs_txt)
sfs_path.write_text(sfs_txt, encoding="latin-1")

# --- 3. En libs/video/rsx_d3d12_backend.c: sincronizar Present() con cellGcm_flip_request_count() ---
d3d_path = Path(r"C:\AsuraPS3\ps3recomp\libs\video\rsx_d3d12_backend.c")
txt = d3d_path.read_text(encoding="latin-1")

old_flip_gate = """    {
        extern unsigned cellGcm_flip_request_count(void);
        if (rsx_present_flip_already_rendered(
                s_clear_presents, s_last_clear_presented_flip,
                cellGcm_flip_request_count()))
            return;
    }"""

new_flip_gate = """    {
        extern unsigned cellGcm_flip_request_count(void);
        static unsigned s_last_flip_present = 0;
        unsigned cur_fc = cellGcm_flip_request_count();
        if (rsx_present_flip_already_rendered(
                s_clear_presents, s_last_clear_presented_flip, cur_fc))
            return;
        /* Wait until the guest actually issues a FLIP before draining/presenting the frame,
         * so 60Hz frame_clock never splits a 30FPS guest frame into two half-built frames! */
        if (cur_fc != 0 && cur_fc == s_last_flip_present && s_d3d.draw_count < (MAX_DRAWS - 256))
            return;
        s_last_flip_present = cur_fc;
    }"""

if "s_last_flip_present" not in txt:
    assert old_flip_gate in txt, "old_flip_gate not found"
    txt = txt.replace(old_flip_gate, new_flip_gate, 1)
    d3d_path.write_text(txt, encoding="latin-1")
    print("[patch] rsx_d3d12_backend_present: sincronizado con el FLIP real del juego (cero parpadeo)!")


==================== FILE: C:\AsuraPS3\out\fix_flicker_root.py ====================

from pathlib import Path

d3d_path = Path(r"C:\AsuraPS3\ps3recomp\libs\video\rsx_d3d12_backend.c")
txt = d3d_path.read_text(encoding="latin-1")

# --- 1. En d3d12_clear: si (mask & 0xF0) == 0 (clear de solo Depth/Stencil a mitad de frame),
#        NO partir el frame ni borrar draw_count; solo encolar el clear de Depth/Stencil!
#        Y si es un clear de Color (mask & 0xF0), NUNCA llamar a render_frame() desde d3d12_clear
#        (solo reiniciar draw_count = 0 si el frame anterior ya fue presentado por FLIP). ---
old_clear_block = """    if (s_d3d.draw_count > 0) {
        int has_disp = 0;
        for (u32 _i = 0; _i < s_d3d.draw_count; _i++)
            if (s_d3d.draws[_i].rt_off == 0) { has_disp = 1; break; }
        if (!has_disp) {
            if (s_d3d.clear_Count < MAX_CLEARS) {
                ClearCmd* cc = &s_d3d.clears[s_d3d.clear_count++];
                cc->rt_off = 0; cc->before_draw = s_d3d.draw_count;
                cc->mask = mask;
                cc->r = r; cc->g = g; cc->b = b; cc->a = a;
            }
            s_d3d.clear_r = r; s_d3d.clear_g = g;
            s_d3d.clear_b = b; s_d3d.clear_a = a;
            s_d3d.has_clear = 1;
            return;
        }
        { static int accum = -1;
          if (accum < 0) { const char* e = getenv("RSX_ACCUM_FRAME"); accum = e ? atoi(e) : 0; }
          if (accum) return; }
        if (s_d3d.initialized) {
            if (blink_dbg())
                printf("[CLEAR] presenting %u accumulated draws at frame boundary\\n",
                       s_d3d.draw_count);
            render_frame();
            s_clear_presents++;
            {
                extern unsigned cellGcm_flip_request_count(void);
                s_last_clear_presented_flip = cellGcm_flip_request_count();
            }
        }
        s_d3d.draw_count = 0;
        s_d3d.clear_count = 0;
    }"""

# Nota: en rsx_d3d12_backend.c es s_d3d.clear_count (minuscula)
old_clear_block = old_clear_block.replace("s_d3d.clear_Count", "s_d3d.clear_count")

new_clear_block = """    if (s_d3d.draw_count > 0) {
        /* If this is a Depth/Stencil-only clear ((mask & 0xF0) == 0) mid-frame before drawing UI,
         * record it in-place and NEVER split or wipe the color draws of the current frame! */
        if ((mask & 0xF0u) == 0) {
            if (s_d3d.clear_count < MAX_CLEARS) {
                ClearCmd* cc = &s_d3d.clears[s_d3d.clear_count++];
                cc->rt_off = 0; cc->before_draw = s_d3d.draw_count;
                cc->mask = mask;
                cc->r = r; cc->g = g; cc->b = b; cc->a = a;
            }
            return;
        }
        /* Color clear on display surface: record it in-place if we haven't flipped yet */
        if (s_d3d.clear_count < MAX_CLEARS) {
            ClearCmd* cc = &s_d3d.clears[s_d3d.clear_count++];
            cc->rt_off = 0; cc->before_draw = s_d3d.draw_count;
            cc->mask = mask;
            cc->r = r; cc->g = g; cc->b = b; cc->a = a;
        }
        return;
    }"""

assert old_clear_block in txt, "old_clear_block not found in rsx_d3d12_backend.c"
txt = txt.replace(old_clear_block, new_clear_block, 1)
d3d_path.write_text(txt, encoding="latin-1")
print("[patch] d3d12_clear corregido: los clears Depth/Stencil de mitad de frame ya no parten ni hacen parpadear la pantalla!")

        return;"""

    dtxt = dtxt.replace(old_dclear, new_dclear, 1)

            return;
        s_last_flip_present = cur_fc;"""

    dtxt = dtxt.replace(old_pres_gate, new_pres_gate, 1)

                                             unsigned last_clear_flip,
                                             unsigned cur_flip_count)
{
    return clear_presents > 0 && cur_flip_count == last_clear_flip;
}"""

new_fn = """static int rsx_present_flip_already_rendered(unsigned clear_presents,
                                             unsigned last_clear_flip,
                                             unsigned cur_flip_count)
{
    if (clear_presents > 0 && s_d3d.has_clear) return 1;
    return clear_presents > 0 && cur_flip_count == last_clear_flip;
}"""

if old_fn in txt:
    txt = txt.replace(old_fn, new_fn, 1)
    print("[patch] rsx_present_flip_already_rendered: bloqueado robo de draws a mitad de frame!")

# Y al terminar render_frame(), apagar has_clear para que si luego viene un video Bink puro sin clear, present() lo muestre
old_rf_end = "    s_d3d.draw_count = 0;\n    s_d3d.clear_count = 0;"
new_rf_end = "    s_d3d.draw_count = 0;\n    s_d3d.clear_count = 0;\n    s_d3d.has_clear = 0;"
